MINIMAL RETENTION POLICY (PROPOSED, NOT INSTALLED)
1. One concise receipt per accepted release: boundary/runtime SHA, deploy IDs,
   acceptance result, checks actually run, rollback refs, and links to selected
   evidence. Retain failure/stopped gate history and important security/data proof.
2. Keep only selected sanitized immutable evidence under project-memory/evidence.
   Maintain a manifest mapping retained files to reports, SHA/checksum and purpose.
   Do not blanket-ignore evidence or auto-commit agent output. Referenced JSON may
   be compacted only after review and link/schema compatibility are handled.
3. Raw logs, repetitive JSON, screenshots, exports and caches belong in a task-
   scoped external scratch directory. At task closure retain representative proof,
   archive valuable unique raw evidence with a checksum, and propose residual
   scratch deletion. Never overwrite an earlier receipt/revision silently.
4. Use external sibling worktrees for future candidates, not nested project trees.
   Log each task's files, ports/PIDs, worktree and retention disposition. Prefer
   existing reusable verifier/support code over another copied release harness.
5. A documentation-only follow-up may adopt this policy in AGENTS.md and tooling,
   plus explicit narrowly scoped output conventions. Approval required first; no
   .gitignore or VS Code setting was changed here. Existing tmp/output exclusions
   are documented rather than expanded.

ONE PROPOSED CLEANUP PLAN — REQUIRES CHRIS APPROVAL
A. Preserve first: archive the 233 audit files as coherent, checksummed research
   packages, retaining original/differing revisions and report links. Adopt the 15
   unique root release reports + selected evidence in a dedicated documentation
   change, after privacy/content review; consolidate identical architecture copy.
   Preserve dirty worktrees, MRP/Itenify research, backups and all unmatched patches.
   This can remove all 266 pre-existing root untracked entries from the workspace
   by reviewed retention/archival, not by declaring 266 disposable. Report is extra.
B. With approved server owners, stop the five identified candidate review servers;
   preserve/reuse local database services unless separately approved. Then remove
   specifically listed regenerated .next/out/out-tenops caches from completed
   candidates. Potential ~17 GB cache recovery, plus ~1.1 GB exports, without touching
   source/ref history. Exclude anything still serving an active candidate.
C. Remove approved clean completed release worktrees whose HEAD is an ancestor or
   fully patch-equivalent, after recording their ignored/private evidence and
   archiving any unique harness dependencies. Keep branch refs initially. Prune the
   27 stale registrations only after checking their external paths/recovery record.
   Do not infer old dirty content from surviving refs. Exact worktree state below.
D. In a later explicit batch, archive redundant raw evidence and exact duplicate
   audit files; rewrite links, manifests and checksums together. No blanket branch
   deletion: retain all rollback/archive/records refs and 29 unmatched branch tips.
   Branch deletion, if ever desired, needs an exact approved list and recovery refs.
E. After cleanup, switch the root to canonical dev or create a dedicated external
   Group Messaging worktree from 64ded3e. Verify clean status, isolate feature work,
   and reclaim port 3000 only with approval of its confirmed review owner. Group
   Messaging must not start from detached 051f23b or absorb historical candidates.
